\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[{\bm{w}}_{\tau}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}]=0
,
\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[\bar{{\bm{w}}}_{\tau}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}]=0
,
\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[z_{\tau+1}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}]=0
, and
{\bm{x}}_{0}=0
due to Assumption
2
. Next, we estimate the conditional auto-correlation function of the effective noise process
\{\zeta_{\tau+1}\}_{\tau=L}^{T-1}
, as follows,

\displaystyle\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\big[\zeta_{\tau+1}\zeta_{\tau^{\prime}+1}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}\big]

\displaystyle=\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\big[(\bar{{\bm{w}}}_{\tau}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\textup{{vec}}({\bm{F}})+{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{e}}_{\tau+1}+z_{\tau+1})(\bar{{\bm{w}}}_{\tau^{\prime}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\textup{{vec}}({\bm{F}})+{\bm{u}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{e}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}+z_{\tau^{\prime}+1}){~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}\big],

\displaystyle=\textup{{vec}}({\bm{F}})^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\big[(\bar{{\bm{w}}}_{\tau}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1})(\bar{{\bm{w}}}_{\tau^{\prime}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}){~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}\big]\textup{{vec}}({\bm{F}})+\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\big[{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{e}}_{\tau+1}{\bm{e}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{u}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}\big]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\big[z_{\tau+1}z_{\tau^{\prime}+1}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}\big]+\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\big[{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{e}}_{\tau+1}(\bar{{\bm{w}}}_{\tau^{\prime}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}){~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}\big]\textup{{vec}}({\bm{F}})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\big[{\bm{u}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{e}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}(\bar{{\bm{w}}}_{\tau}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}){~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}\big]\textup{{vec}}({\bm{F}})+\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\big[z_{\tau+1}(\bar{{\bm{w}}}_{\tau^{\prime}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}){~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}\big]\textup{{vec}}({\bm{F}})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\big[z_{\tau^{\prime}+1}(\bar{{\bm{w}}}_{\tau}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}){~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}\big]\textup{{vec}}({\bm{F}})+\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\big[z_{\tau+1}{\bm{u}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{e}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}\big]+\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\big[z_{\tau^{\prime}+1}{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{e}}_{\tau+1}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}\big].

(A.2)

In the following, we will calculate each term in (
A.2
) separately to obtain an upper bound on the conditional auto-covariance
\mathcal{R}_{\zeta}[\tau,\tau^{\prime}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}]
.

\bullet

First term in (
A.2
):
This term captures the conditional auto-correlation of the noise process
\{\bar{{\bm{w}}}_{\tau}\}_{\tau=L}^{T-1}
. Using Assumption
2
, this term takes the following form,

\displaystyle\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\big[(\bar{{\bm{w}}}_{\tau}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1})(\bar{{\bm{w}}}_{\tau^{\prime}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}){~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}\big]

\displaystyle=\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\big[\bar{{\bm{w}}}_{\tau}\bar{{\bm{w}}}_{\tau^{\prime}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}{\bm{u}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}\big],

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(a)}}}{{=}}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\big[\bar{{\bm{w}}}_{\tau}\bar{{\bm{w}}}_{\tau^{\prime}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}\big]\otimes\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\big[{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}{\bm{u}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}\big],

\displaystyle\;=\;\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\big[\bar{{\bm{w}}}_{\tau}\bar{{\bm{w}}}_{\tau^{\prime}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}\big]\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}{\bm{u}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}},

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(b)}}}{{=}}\bar{\bm{\updelta}}_{w}(\tau,\tau^{\prime})\otimes{\bm{\Sigma}}_{w}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}{\bm{u}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}},

(A.3)

where we get (a) from the independence of
\bar{{\bm{w}}}_{\tau}\bar{{\bm{w}}}_{\tau^{\prime}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}
and
{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}{\bm{u}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}
, (b) from Assumption
2
and defining a Toeplitz matrix
\bar{\bm{\updelta}}_{w}(\tau,\tau^{\prime})
of Kronecker delta functions
\delta(\cdot)
as follows,

\displaystyle\bar{\bm{\updelta}}_{w}(\tau,\tau^{\prime}):=\begin{bmatrix}\delta(\tau-\tau^{\prime})&\delta(\tau-\tau^{\prime}+1)&\ldots&\delta(\tau-\tau^{\prime}+L-1)\\
\delta(\tau-\tau^{\prime}-1)&\delta(\tau-\tau^{\prime})&\ldots&\delta(\tau-\tau^{\prime}+L-2)\\
\vdots&\vdots&\ddots&\vdots\\
\delta(\tau-\tau^{\prime}-L+1)&\delta(\tau-\tau^{\prime}-L+2)&\ldots&\delta(\tau-\tau^{\prime})\end{bmatrix}\in\mathbb{R}^{L\times L}.

(A.4)

Using the above definition, we observe that
\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\big[\bar{{\bm{w}}}_{\tau}\bar{{\bm{w}}}_{\tau^{\prime}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}\big]
is an
nL\times nL
block Toeplitz matrix with the
ij
-th
n\times n
block given by
\delta(\tau-\tau^{\prime}-i+j){\bm{\Sigma}}_{w}
. This further implies that
\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\big[(\bar{{\bm{w}}}_{\tau}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1})(\bar{{\bm{w}}}_{\tau^{\prime}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}){~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}\big]
is an
npL\times npL
block Toeplitz matrix with the
ij
-th
np\times np
block given by
\delta(\tau-\tau^{\prime}-i+j){\bm{\Sigma}}_{w}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}{\bm{u}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}
.

\bullet

Second term in (
A.2
):
This term captures the conditional auto-correlation of the error process
\{{\bm{e}}_{\tau+1}\}_{\tau=L}^{T-1}
which quantifies the effect of unknown state at time
\tau-L+1
. This term can be calculated as follows,

\displaystyle\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\big[{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{e}}_{\tau+1}{\bm{e}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{u}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}\big]

\displaystyle={\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\big[{\bm{e}}_{\tau+1}{\bm{e}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}\big]{\bm{u}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1},

\displaystyle={\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{C}}{\bm{A}}^{L}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\big[{\bm{x}}_{\tau-L+1}{\bm{x}}_{\tau^{\prime}-L+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}\big]({\bm{A}}^{L})^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{C}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{u}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}.

(A.5)

To proceed, from (
2.1
) we have

\displaystyle\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[{\bm{x}}_{t}{\bm{x}}_{t^{\prime}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}]

\displaystyle=\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\bigg[\big(\sum_{i=0}^{t-1}{\bm{A}}^{t-i-1}{\bm{B}}{\bm{u}}_{i}+\sum_{i=0}^{t-1}{\bm{A}}^{t-i-1}{\bm{w}}_{i}\big)\big(\sum_{j=0}^{t^{\prime}-1}{\bm{A}}^{t^{\prime}-j-1}{\bm{B}}{\bm{u}}_{j}+\sum_{j=0}^{t^{\prime}-1}{\bm{A}}^{t^{\prime}-j-1}{\bm{w}}_{j}\big)^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}\bigg],

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=0}^{t-1}\sum_{j=0}^{t^{\prime}-1}{\bm{A}}^{t-i-1}{\bm{B}}{\bm{u}}_{i}{\bm{u}}_{j}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{B}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}({\bm{A}}^{t^{\prime}-j-1})^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}+\sum_{i=0}^{t-1}\sum_{j=0}^{t^{\prime}-1}{\bm{A}}^{t-i-1}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[{\bm{w}}_{i}{\bm{w}}_{j}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}]({\bm{A}}^{t^{\prime}-j-1})^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}},

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(a)}}}{{=}}\sum_{i=0}^{t-1}\sum_{j=0}^{t^{\prime}-1}{\bm{A}}^{t-i-1}{\bm{B}}{\bm{u}}_{i}{\bm{u}}_{j}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{B}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}({\bm{A}}^{t^{\prime}-j-1})^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}+\sum_{i=0}^{\min\{t,t^{\prime}\}-1}{\bm{A}}^{t-i-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}_{w}({\bm{A}}^{t^{\prime}-i-1})^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}},

(A.6)

where we obtain (a) by using Assumption
2
. Hence, we have

\displaystyle\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\big[{\bm{x}}_{\tau-L+1}{\bm{x}}_{\tau^{\prime}-L+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}\big]=\sum_{i=0}^{\tau-L}\sum_{j=0}^{\tau^{\prime}-L}{\bm{A}}^{\tau-L-i}{\bm{B}}{\bm{u}}_{i}{\bm{u}}_{j}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{B}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}({\bm{A}}^{\tau^{\prime}-L-j})^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}+\sum_{i=0}^{\min\{\tau,\tau^{\prime}\}-L}{\bm{A}}^{\tau-L-i}{\bm{\Sigma}}_{w}({\bm{A}}^{\tau^{\prime}-L-i})^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}},

(A.7)

Plugging this back into (
A.5
), we obtain the following,

\displaystyle\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\big[{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{e}}_{\tau+1}{\bm{e}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{u}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}\big]

\displaystyle\quad\quad={\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{C}}{\bm{A}}^{L}\bigg[\sum_{i=0}^{\tau-L}\sum_{j=0}^{\tau^{\prime}-L}{\bm{A}}^{\tau-L-i}{\bm{B}}{\bm{u}}_{i}{\bm{u}}_{j}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{B}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}({\bm{A}}^{\tau^{\prime}-L-j})^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}+\sum_{i=0}^{\min\{\tau,\tau^{\prime}\}-L}{\bm{A}}^{\tau-L-i}{\bm{\Sigma}}_{w}({\bm{A}}^{\tau^{\prime}-L-i})^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\bigg]({\bm{A}}^{L})^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{C}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{u}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1},

\displaystyle\quad\quad={\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\bigg[\sum_{i=0}^{\tau-L}\sum_{j=0}^{\tau^{\prime}-L}{\bm{C}}{\bm{A}}^{\tau-i}{\bm{B}}{\bm{u}}_{i}{\bm{u}}_{j}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{B}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}({\bm{A}}^{\tau^{\prime}-j})^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{C}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}+\sum_{i=0}^{\min\{\tau,\tau^{\prime}\}-L}{\bm{C}}{\bm{A}}^{\tau-i}{\bm{\Sigma}}_{w}({\bm{A}}^{\tau^{\prime}-i})^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{C}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\bigg]{\bm{u}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}.

(A.8)

\bullet

Third term in (
A.2
):
This term captures the conditional auto-correlation of the measurement noise process
\{z_{\tau+1}\}_{\tau=L}^{T-1}
. Using Assumption
2
, it is very straightforward to see that

\displaystyle\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\big[z_{\tau+1}z_{\tau^{\prime}+1}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}\big]=\sigma_{z}^{2}\delta(\tau-\tau^{\prime}).

(A.9)

The remaining terms in (
A.2
) captures the cross-correlation between various noise terms, and are calculated in the following

\bullet

Fourth & Fifth terms in (
A.2
):
These two terms capture the conditional cross-correlation between the error process
\{{\bm{e}}_{\tau+1}\}_{\tau=L}^{T-1}
and the noise process
\{\bar{{\bm{w}}}_{\tau}\}_{\tau=L}^{T-1}
. To begin, considering the fourth term in (
A.2
), we have

\displaystyle\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\big[{\bm{u}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{e}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}(\bar{{\bm{w}}}_{\tau}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}){~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}\big]

\displaystyle=\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\big[{\bm{u}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{e}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}\bar{{\bm{w}}}_{\tau}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}\big],

\displaystyle={\bm{u}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{C}}{\bm{A}}^{L}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\big[{\bm{x}}_{\tau^{\prime}-L+1}\bar{{\bm{w}}}_{\tau}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}\big]\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}.

(A.10)

To proceed, from (
2.1
) we have

\displaystyle\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\big[{\bm{x}}_{t^{\prime}}\bar{{\bm{w}}}_{t}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}\big]

\displaystyle\,=\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\big[\big(\sum_{i=0}^{t^{\prime}-1}{\bm{A}}^{t^{\prime}-i-1}{\bm{B}}{\bm{u}}_{i}+\sum_{i=0}^{t^{\prime}-1}{\bm{A}}^{t^{\prime}-i-1}{\bm{w}}_{i}\big)\bar{{\bm{w}}}_{t}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}\big],

\displaystyle\,=\sum_{i=0}^{t^{\prime}-1}{\bm{A}}^{t^{\prime}-i-1}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\big[{\bm{w}}_{i}\bar{{\bm{w}}}_{t}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}\big],

\displaystyle\,=\sum_{i=0}^{t^{\prime}-1}{\bm{A}}^{t^{\prime}-i-1}\big({\bm{\updelta}}_{w}(t,i)\otimes{\bm{\Sigma}}_{w}\big),

(A.11)

where we define
{\bm{\updelta}}_{w}(t,i):=\begin{bmatrix}\delta(t-i)&\delta(t-i-1)&\ldots&\delta(t-i-L+1)\end{bmatrix}\in\mathbb{R}^{1\times L}
. Note that
{\bm{\updelta}}_{w}(t,i)=[1~0~\cdots~0]
when
i=t
, and
{\bm{\updelta}}_{w}(t,i)=[0~\cdots~0~1]
when
i=t-L+1
. Specifically,
{\bm{\updelta}}_{w}(t,i)
has all zero entries except the
(t-i)
-th entry when
i\in[t-L+1,t]
, and it has all zero entries when
i\notin[t-L+1,t]
. Plugging this back into (
A.10
), we have

\displaystyle\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\big[{\bm{u}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{e}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}(\bar{{\bm{w}}}_{\tau}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}){~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}\big]

\displaystyle=\big({\bm{u}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{C}}{\bm{A}}^{L}\sum_{i=0}^{\tau^{\prime}-L}{\bm{A}}^{\tau^{\prime}-L-i}\big({\bm{\updelta}}_{w}(\tau,i)\otimes{\bm{\Sigma}}_{w}\big)\big)\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}.

(A.12)

Using a similar line of reasoning, the fifth term in (
A.2
) can also be calculated as,

\displaystyle\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\big[{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{e}}_{\tau+1}(\bar{{\bm{w}}}_{\tau^{\prime}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}){~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}\big]

\displaystyle=\big({\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{C}}{\bm{A}}^{L}\sum_{i=0}^{\tau-L}{\bm{A}}^{\tau-L-i}\big({\bm{\updelta}}_{w}(\tau^{\prime},i)\otimes{\bm{\Sigma}}_{w}\big)\big)\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}.

(A.13)

\bullet

Remaining terms in (
A.2
):

The last four terms in (
A.9
) captures the conditional cross-correlation between the measurement noise process
\{z_{\tau+1}\}_{\tau=L}^{T-1}
and the rest of noise/error processes
\{\bar{{\bm{w}}}_{\tau}\}_{\tau=L}^{T-1}
,
\{{\bm{e}}_{\tau+1}\}_{\tau=L}^{T-1}
. Because the measurement noise process is independent of the rest of noise/error processes due to Assumption
2
, all of these terms are zero,

\displaystyle

\displaystyle\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\big[z_{\tau+1}(\bar{{\bm{w}}}_{\tau^{\prime}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}){~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}\big]=\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\big[z_{\tau^{\prime}+1}(\bar{{\bm{w}}}_{\tau}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}){~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}\big]=0,

(A.14)

\displaystyle\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\big[z_{\tau+1}{\bm{u}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{e}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}\big]=\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\big[z_{\tau^{\prime}+1}{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{e}}_{\tau+1}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}\big]=0.

\blacksquare

Finalizing the proof:
Finally plugging (
A.3
), (
A.8
), (
A.9
), (
A.12
), (
A.13
), and (
A.14
) into (
A.2
), and combining the result with (
A.1
), we obtain the following expression for the conditional auto-covariance of the of the effective noise process
\{\zeta_{\tau+1}\}_{\tau=L}^{T-1}
,

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{\zeta}[\tau,\tau^{\prime}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}]

\displaystyle:=\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\big[\zeta_{\tau+1}\zeta_{\tau^{\prime}+1}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}\big]-\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\big[\zeta_{\tau+1}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}\big]\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\big[\zeta_{\tau^{\prime}+1}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}\big]

\displaystyle=\textup{{vec}}({\bm{F}})^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\big(\bar{\bm{\updelta}}_{w}(\tau,\tau^{\prime})\otimes{\bm{\Sigma}}_{w}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}{\bm{u}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\big)\textup{{vec}}({\bm{F}})

\displaystyle+{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\sum_{i=0}^{\tau-L}\sum_{j=0}^{\tau^{\prime}-L}{\bm{C}}{\bm{A}}^{\tau-i}{\bm{B}}{\bm{u}}_{i}{\bm{u}}_{j}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{B}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}({\bm{A}}^{\tau^{\prime}-j})^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{C}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{u}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}

\displaystyle+{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\sum_{i=0}^{\min\{\tau,\tau^{\prime}\}-L}{\bm{C}}{\bm{A}}^{\tau-i}{\bm{\Sigma}}_{w}({\bm{A}}^{\tau^{\prime}-i})^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{C}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{u}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}+\sigma_{z}^{2}\delta(\tau-\tau^{\prime})

\displaystyle+\big[\big({\bm{u}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{C}}{\bm{A}}^{L}\sum_{i=0}^{\tau^{\prime}-L}{\bm{A}}^{\tau^{\prime}-L-i}\big({\bm{\updelta}}_{w}(\tau,i)\otimes{\bm{\Sigma}}_{w}\big)\big)\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\big]\textup{{vec}}({\bm{F}}),

\displaystyle+\big[\big({\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{C}}{\bm{A}}^{L}\sum_{i=0}^{\tau-L}{\bm{A}}^{\tau-L-i}\big({\bm{\updelta}}_{w}(\tau^{\prime},i)\otimes{\bm{\Sigma}}_{w}\big)\big)\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\big]\textup{{vec}}({\bm{F}}),

\displaystyle-{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\sum_{i=0}^{\tau-L}\sum_{j=0}^{\tau^{\prime}-L}{\bm{C}}{\bm{A}}^{\tau-i}{\bm{B}}{\bm{u}}_{i}{\bm{u}}_{j}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{B}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}({\bm{A}}^{\tau^{\prime}-j})^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{C}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{u}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1},

\displaystyle=\textup{{vec}}({\bm{F}})^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\big(\bar{\bm{\updelta}}_{w}(\tau,\tau^{\prime})\otimes{\bm{\Sigma}}_{w}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}{\bm{u}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\big)\textup{{vec}}({\bm{F}})

\displaystyle+{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\sum_{i=0}^{\min\{\tau,\tau^{\prime}\}-L}{\bm{C}}{\bm{A}}^{\tau-i}{\bm{\Sigma}}_{w}({\bm{A}}^{\tau^{\prime}-i})^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{C}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{u}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}+\sigma_{z}^{2}\delta(\tau-\tau^{\prime})

\displaystyle+\big[\big({\bm{u}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{C}}{\bm{A}}^{L}\sum_{i=0}^{\tau^{\prime}-L}{\bm{A}}^{\tau^{\prime}-L-i}\big({\bm{\updelta}}_{w}(\tau,i)\otimes{\bm{\Sigma}}_{w}\big)\big)\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\big]\textup{{vec}}({\bm{F}}),

\displaystyle+\big[\big({\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{C}}{\bm{A}}^{L}\sum_{i=0}^{\tau-L}{\bm{A}}^{\tau-L-i}\big({\bm{\updelta}}_{w}(\tau^{\prime},i)\otimes{\bm{\Sigma}}_{w}\big)\big)\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\big]\textup{{vec}}({\bm{F}}).

(A.15)

This completes the proof.

A.2
Proof of Theorem
6.2

Proof

To begin, recalling the input-output relation
y_{t}=\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{t-1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{t}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\textup{{vec}}({\bm{G}})+\zeta_{t}
, where
\zeta_{t}=\bar{{\bm{w}}}_{t-1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{t}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\textup{{vec}}({\bm{F}})+{\bm{u}}_{t}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{e}}_{t}+z_{t}
, the estimation error is alternately given by the following relation,

\displaystyle\Delta{\bm{G}}:=\textup{{vec}}(\hat{{\bm{G}}})-\textup{{vec}}({\bm{G}})

\displaystyle=\big(\sum_{\tau=L}^{T-1}\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\big)^{-1}\sum_{\tau=L}^{T-1}(\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1})\zeta_{\tau+1}.

(A.16)

Hence, the conditional covariance of the estimation error is given by,

\displaystyle{\bm{\Sigma}}[\Delta{\bm{G}}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}]

\displaystyle=\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[\Delta{\bm{G}}\Delta{\bm{G}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}]-\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[\Delta{\bm{G}}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}]\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[\Delta{\bm{G}}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}]^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}},

\displaystyle=\big(\sum_{\tau=L}^{T-1}\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\big)^{-1}\bigg(\sum_{\tau=L}^{T-1}\sum_{\tau^{\prime}=L}^{T-1}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\big[\zeta_{\tau+1}\zeta_{\tau^{\prime}+1}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}\big]\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau^{\prime}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}{\bm{u}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\quad\big(\sum_{\tau=L}^{T-1}\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\big)^{-1}

\displaystyle-\big(\sum_{\tau=L}^{T-1}\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\big)^{-1}\bigg(\sum_{\tau=L}^{T-1}\sum_{\tau^{\prime}=L}^{T-1}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\big[\zeta_{\tau+1}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}\big]\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\big[\zeta_{\tau^{\prime}+1}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}\big]\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau^{\prime}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}{\bm{u}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\quad\big(\sum_{\tau=L}^{T-1}\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\big)^{-1},

\displaystyle=\big(\sum_{\tau=L}^{T-1}\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\big)^{-1}\bigg(\sum_{\tau=L}^{T-1}\sum_{\tau^{\prime}=L}^{T-1}\mathcal{R}_{\zeta}[\tau,\tau^{\prime}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}]\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau^{\prime}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}{\bm{u}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\quad\big(\sum_{\tau=L}^{T-1}\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\big)^{-1},

(A.17)

where
\mathcal{R}_{\zeta}[\tau,\tau^{\prime}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}]
denotes the conditional auto-covariance function of the effective noise process
\{\zeta_{\tau+1}\}_{\tau=L}^{T-1}
, and it is as given in Lemma
6.1
. Therefore, in order to upper bound the conditional covariance
{\bm{\Sigma}}[\Delta{\bm{G}}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}]
, it suffices to upper bound the summation
\sum_{\tau=L}^{T-1}\sum_{\tau^{\prime}=L}^{T-1}\mathcal{R}_{\zeta}[\tau,\tau^{\prime}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}]\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau^{\prime}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}{\bm{u}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}
. To do that, we first use the fact that
\mathcal{R}_{\zeta}[\tau,\tau^{\prime}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}]=\mathcal{R}_{\zeta}[\tau^{\prime},\tau{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}]
due to symmetry, and the observation that for any two vectors
{\bm{v}}_{1}
and
{\bm{v}}_{2}
of appropriate dimensions we have
{\bm{v}}_{1}{\bm{v}}_{2}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}+{\bm{v}}_{2}{\bm{v}}_{1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\preceq{\bm{v}}_{1}{\bm{v}}_{1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}+{\bm{v}}_{2}{\bm{v}}_{2}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}
, to obtain the following upper bound,

\displaystyle\sum_{\tau=L}^{T-1}\sum_{\tau^{\prime}=L}^{T-1}\mathcal{R}_{\zeta}[\tau,\tau^{\prime}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}]\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau^{\prime}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}{\bm{u}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}

\displaystyle\preceq\sum_{\tau=L}^{T-1}\mathcal{R}_{\zeta}[\tau,\tau{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}]\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\tau=L}^{T-1}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\tau^{\prime}=L\\
\tau^{\prime}\neq\tau\end{subarray}}^{T-1}\big|\mathcal{R}_{\zeta}[\tau,\tau^{\prime}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}]\big|\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}.

(A.18)

The first term on the RHS of (
A.18
), can be upper bounded using Lemma (
6.1
) and (
6.5
) as follows,

\displaystyle\sum_{\tau=L}^{T-1}\mathcal{R}_{\zeta}[\tau,\tau{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}]\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\preceq\big(\big(\|{\bm{\Sigma}}_{w}\|\|{{\bm{F}}}\|_{F}^{2}+\|{\bm{C}}{\bm{A}}^{L}\|^{2}\|{\bm{\Gamma}}_{w}^{\infty}\|\big)\beta^{2}+\sigma_{z}^{2}\big)\sum_{\tau=L}^{T-1}\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}.

(A.19)

Similarly, we use Lemma
6.1
to upper bound the second term on the RHS of (
A.18
). We have,

\displaystyle\sum_{\tau=L}^{T-1}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\tau^{\prime}=L\\
\tau^{\prime}\neq\tau\end{subarray}}^{T-1}

\displaystyle\big|\mathcal{R}_{\zeta}[\tau,\tau^{\prime}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}]\big|\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}

\displaystyle\preceq\sum_{\tau=L}^{T-1}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\tau^{\prime}=L\\
\tau^{\prime}\neq\tau\end{subarray}}^{T-1}\bigg[\big|\textup{{vec}}({\bm{F}})^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\big(\bar{\bm{\updelta}}_{w}(\tau,\tau^{\prime})\otimes{\bm{\Sigma}}_{w}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}{\bm{u}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\big)\textup{{vec}}({\bm{F}})\big|

\displaystyle+\big|\sum_{i=0}^{\min\{\tau,\tau^{\prime}\}-L}{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{C}}{\bm{A}}^{\tau-i}{\bm{\Sigma}}_{w}({\bm{A}}^{\tau^{\prime}-i})^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{C}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{u}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}\big|

\displaystyle+\big|\big[\big({\bm{u}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{C}}{\bm{A}}^{L}\sum_{i=0}^{\tau^{\prime}-L}{\bm{A}}^{\tau^{\prime}-L-i}\big({\bm{\updelta}}_{w}(\tau,i)\otimes{\bm{\Sigma}}_{w}\big)\big)\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\big]\textup{{vec}}({\bm{F}})\big|

\displaystyle+\big|\big[\big({\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{C}}{\bm{A}}^{L}\sum_{i=0}^{\tau-L}{\bm{A}}^{\tau-L-i}\big({\bm{\updelta}}_{w}(\tau^{\prime},i)\otimes{\bm{\Sigma}}_{w}\big)\big)\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\big]\textup{{vec}}({\bm{F}})\big|\bigg]\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}.

(A.20)

In the following, we will upper bound each summation in (
A.20
) separately to obtain an upper bound on the conditional covariance
{\bm{\Sigma}}[\Delta{\bm{G}}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}]
.

\bullet

First summation in (
A.20
):
This summation can be upper bounded as follows,

\displaystyle\sum_{\tau=L}^{T-1}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\tau^{\prime}=L\\
\tau^{\prime}\neq\tau\end{subarray}}^{T-1}

\displaystyle\big|\textup{{vec}}({\bm{F}})^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\big(\bar{\bm{\updelta}}_{w}(\tau,\tau^{\prime})\otimes{\bm{\Sigma}}_{w}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}{\bm{u}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\big)\textup{{vec}}({\bm{F}})\big|\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(i)}}}{{\preceq}}\sum_{\tau=L}^{T-1}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\tau^{\prime}=\max\{L,\tau-L+1\}\\
\tau^{\prime}\neq\tau\end{subarray}}^{\min\{T-1,\tau+L-1\}}\big(\|\bar{\bm{\updelta}}_{w}(\tau,\tau^{\prime})\|\|{\bm{\Sigma}}_{w}\|\|{{\bm{F}}}\|_{F}^{2}\|{{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}}\|_{\ell_{2}}\|{{\bm{u}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}}\|_{\ell_{2}}\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}\big)\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}},

\displaystyle\preceq\|{\bm{\Sigma}}_{w}\|\|{{\bm{F}}}\|_{F}^{2}\beta^{2}\sum_{\tau=L}^{T-1}\bigg[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\tau^{\prime}=\max\{L,\tau-L+1\}\\
\tau^{\prime}\neq\tau\end{subarray}}^{\min\{T-1,\tau+L-1\}}\|\bar{\bm{\updelta}}_{w}(\tau,\tau^{\prime})\|\bigg]\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}},

\displaystyle\preceq 2\|{\bm{\Sigma}}_{w}\|\|{{\bm{F}}}\|_{F}^{2}\beta^{2}L\sum_{\tau=L}^{T-1}\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}},

(A.21)

where we obtain (i) from the following observation: recalling the Toeplitz matrix
\bar{\bm{\updelta}}_{w}(\tau,\tau^{\prime})
from (
6.2
), we can upper bound its spectral norm as follows:

(
{\bm{a}}
)

When
\tau^{\prime}=\tau
, the matrix
\bar{\bm{\updelta}}_{w}(\tau,\tau^{\prime})
becomes an identity matrix
{\bm{I}}_{L}
with
\|\bar{\bm{\updelta}}_{w}(\tau,\tau^{\prime})\|=1

(
{\bm{b}}
)

When
\tau-L<\tau^{\prime}<\tau
, the matrix
\bar{\bm{\updelta}}_{w}(\tau,\tau^{\prime})
becomes a lower shift matrix with
i,j
-th element given by
[\bar{\bm{\updelta}}_{w}(\tau,\tau^{\prime})]_{i,j}=\delta(i-(j+\tau-\tau^{\prime}))
. This further implies
\|\bar{\bm{\updelta}}_{w}(\tau,\tau^{\prime})\|\leq 1
.

(
{\bm{c}}
)

When
\tau<\tau^{\prime}<\tau+L
, the matrix
\bar{\bm{\updelta}}_{w}(\tau,\tau^{\prime})
becomes an upper shift matrix with
i,j
-th element given by
[\bar{\bm{\updelta}}_{w}(\tau,\tau^{\prime})]_{i,j}=\delta((i+\tau^{\prime}-\tau)-j)
. This further implies
\|\bar{\bm{\updelta}}_{w}(\tau,\tau^{\prime})\|\leq 1
.

(
{\bm{d}}
)

When
|\tau-\tau^{\prime}|\geq L
, we have
\bar{\bm{\updelta}}_{w}(\tau,\tau^{\prime})={\bm{0}}
.

\bullet

Second summation in (
A.20
):
Using Assumption
1
, this term can be upper bounded as follows,

\displaystyle\sum_{\tau=L}^{T-1}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\tau^{\prime}=L\\
\tau^{\prime}\neq\tau\end{subarray}}^{T-1}\big|\sum_{i=0}^{\min\{\tau,\tau^{\prime}\}-L}

\displaystyle{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{C}}{\bm{A}}^{\tau-i}{\bm{\Sigma}}_{w}({\bm{A}}^{\tau^{\prime}-i})^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{C}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{u}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}\big|\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(a)}}}{{\preceq}}\sum_{\tau=L}^{T-1}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\tau^{\prime}=L\\
\tau^{\prime}\neq\tau\end{subarray}}^{T-1}\big(\beta^{2}\|{{\bm{C}}{\bm{A}}^{L}}\|^{2}\|{{\bm{\Gamma}}_{w}^{\infty}}\|\|{{\bm{A}}^{|\tau-\tau^{\prime}|}}\|\big)\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}

\displaystyle\preceq\beta^{2}\|{{\bm{C}}{\bm{A}}^{L}}\|^{2}\|{{\bm{\Gamma}}_{w}^{\infty}}\|\phi({\bm{A}},\rho)\sum_{\tau=L}^{T-1}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\tau^{\prime}=L\\
\tau^{\prime}\neq\tau\end{subarray}}^{T-1}\rho^{|\tau-\tau^{\prime}|}\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(b)}}}{{\preceq}}2\beta^{2}\|{{\bm{C}}{\bm{A}}^{L}}\|^{2}\|{{\bm{\Gamma}}_{w}^{\infty}}\|\phi({\bm{A}},\rho)\sum_{\tau=L}^{T-1}\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\sum_{i=1}^{\max\{\begin{subarray}{c}T-\tau-1,\\
\tau-L\end{subarray}\}}\rho^{i},

\displaystyle\preceq 2\beta^{2}\|{{\bm{C}}{\bm{A}}^{L}}\|^{2}\|{{\bm{\Gamma}}_{w}^{\infty}}\|\frac{\rho\phi({\bm{A}},\rho)}{1-\rho}\sum_{\tau=L}^{T-1}\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}},

(A.22)

where we get (a) from the following observation: when
\tau>\tau^{\prime}
, we have

\displaystyle\big|\sum_{i=0}^{\min\{\tau,\tau^{\prime}\}-L}{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{C}}{\bm{A}}^{\tau-i}{\bm{\Sigma}}_{w}({\bm{A}}^{\tau^{\prime}-i})^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{C}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{u}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}\big|

\displaystyle=\big|{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{C}}{\bm{A}}^{\tau-\tau^{\prime}}\big(\sum_{i=0}^{\tau^{\prime}-L}{\bm{A}}^{\tau^{\prime}-i}{\bm{\Sigma}}_{w}({\bm{A}}^{\tau^{\prime}-i})^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\big){\bm{C}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{u}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}\big|,

\displaystyle\leq\big|{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{C}}{\bm{A}}^{L}{\bm{A}}^{\tau-\tau^{\prime}}{\bm{\Gamma}}_{w}^{\infty}({\bm{A}}^{L})^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{C}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{u}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}\big|,

\displaystyle\leq\beta^{2}\|{{\bm{C}}{\bm{A}}^{L}}\|^{2}\|{{\bm{\Gamma}}_{w}^{\infty}}\|\|{{\bm{A}}^{\tau-\tau^{\prime}}}\|.

Similarly, when
\tau<\tau^{\prime}
, we have
\big|\sum_{i=0}^{\min\{\tau,\tau^{\prime}\}-L}{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{C}}{\bm{A}}^{\tau-i}{\bm{\Sigma}}_{w}({\bm{A}}^{\tau^{\prime}-i})^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{C}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{u}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}\big|\leq\beta^{2}\|{{\bm{C}}{\bm{A}}^{L}}\|^{2}\|{{\bm{\Gamma}}_{w}^{\infty}}\|\|{{\bm{A}}^{\tau^{\prime}-\tau}}\|
, using the same line of reasoning. Combining the two cases gives (a). Moreover, (b) is obtained by writing up the summation
\sum_{\tau=L}^{T-1}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\tau^{\prime}=L\\
\tau^{\prime}\neq\tau\end{subarray}}^{T-1}\rho^{|\tau-\tau^{\prime}|}
for each value of
\tau\in[L,T-1]
.

\bullet

Third summation in (
A.20
):
Using Assumptions
1
and
2
, this term can be upper bounded as follows,

\displaystyle\sum_{\tau=L}^{T-1}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\tau^{\prime}=L\\
\tau^{\prime}\neq\tau\end{subarray}}^{T-1}\big|\big[\big({\bm{u}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{C}}{\bm{A}}^{L}

\displaystyle\sum_{i=0}^{\tau^{\prime}-L}{\bm{A}}^{\tau^{\prime}-L-i}\big({\bm{\updelta}}_{w}(\tau,i)\otimes{\bm{\Sigma}}_{w}\big)\big)\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\big]\textup{{vec}}({\bm{F}})\big|\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\tau=L}^{T-1}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\tau^{\prime}=L\\
\tau^{\prime}\neq\tau\end{subarray}}^{T-1}\big|\big({\bm{u}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{C}}{\bm{A}}^{L}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\big[{\bm{x}}_{\tau^{\prime}-L+1}\bar{{\bm{w}}}_{\tau}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}\big]\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\big)\textup{{vec}}({\bm{F}})\big|\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}},

\displaystyle\preceq\beta^{2}\|{{\bm{F}}}\|_{F}\sum_{\tau=L}^{T-1}\bigg[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\tau^{\prime}=L\\
\tau^{\prime}\neq\tau\end{subarray}}^{T-1}\|{\bm{C}}{\bm{A}}^{L}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\big[{\bm{x}}_{\tau^{\prime}-L+1}\bar{{\bm{w}}}_{\tau}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}\big]\|\bigg]\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}},

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(i)}}}{{\preceq}}\beta^{2}\|{{\bm{F}}}\|_{F}^{2}\|{\bm{\Sigma}}_{w}\|(L+\frac{\rho}{1-\rho})\phi({\bm{A}},\rho)\rho^{L}\sum_{\tau=L}^{T-1}\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}},

(A.23)

where we obtain (i) by upper bounding
\|{\bm{C}}{\bm{A}}^{L}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\big[{\bm{x}}_{\tau^{\prime}-L+1}\bar{{\bm{w}}}_{\tau}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}\big]\|
as follows:

({\bm{a}})

When
\tau^{\prime}\leq\tau
, we have
\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\big[{\bm{x}}_{\tau^{\prime}-L+1}\bar{{\bm{w}}}_{\tau}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}\big]={\bm{0}}
. Hence,
\|{\bm{C}}{\bm{A}}^{L}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\big[{\bm{x}}_{\tau^{\prime}-L+1}\bar{{\bm{w}}}_{\tau}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}\big]\|=0

({\bm{b}})

When
\tau<\tau^{\prime}<\tau+L
, we have

\displaystyle\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\big[{\bm{x}}_{\tau^{\prime}-L+1}\bar{{\bm{w}}}_{\tau}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}\big]=\sum_{i=\tau-L+1}^{\tau^{\prime}-L}{\bm{A}}^{\tau^{\prime}-L-i}\big({\bm{\updelta}}_{w}(\tau,i)\otimes{\bm{\Sigma}}_{w}\big)=\begin{bmatrix}{\bm{0}}&\ldots&{\bm{\Sigma}}_{w}&{\bm{A}}{\bm{\Sigma}}_{w}&\ldots&{\bm{A}}^{\tau^{\prime}-\tau-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}_{w}\end{bmatrix}.

\displaystyle\|{\bm{C}}{\bm{A}}^{L}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\big[{\bm{x}}_{\tau^{\prime}-L+1}\bar{{\bm{w}}}_{\tau}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}\big]\|=\|\begin{bmatrix}{\bm{0}}&\ldots&{\bm{C}}({\bm{A}}^{L}{\bm{\Sigma}}_{w})&{\bm{C}}{\bm{A}}({\bm{A}}^{L}{\bm{\Sigma}}_{w})&\ldots&{\bm{C}}{\bm{A}}^{\tau^{\prime}-\tau-1}({\bm{A}}^{L}{\bm{\Sigma}}_{w})\end{bmatrix}\|,

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\;\,\leq\|{\begin{bmatrix}{\bm{0}}&\ldots&{\bm{C}}&{\bm{C}}{\bm{A}}&\ldots&{\bm{C}}{\bm{A}}^{\tau^{\prime}-\tau-1}\end{bmatrix}}\|_{F}\|{\bm{A}}^{L}{\bm{\Sigma}}_{w}\|,

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\;\,\leq\phi({\bm{A}},\rho)\rho^{L}\|{{\bm{\Sigma}}_{w}}\|\|{{\bm{F}}}\|_{F}.

(A.24)

({\bm{c}})

When
\tau^{\prime}=\tau+L
, we have

\displaystyle\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\big[{\bm{x}}_{\tau^{\prime}-L+1}\bar{{\bm{w}}}_{\tau}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}\big]=\sum_{i=\tau-L+1}^{\tau}{\bm{A}}^{\tau-i}\big({\bm{\updelta}}_{w}(\tau,i)\otimes{\bm{\Sigma}}_{w}\big)=\begin{bmatrix}{\bm{\Sigma}}_{w}&{\bm{A}}{\bm{\Sigma}}_{w}&\ldots&{\bm{A}}^{L-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}_{w}\end{bmatrix}.

\displaystyle\|{\bm{C}}{\bm{A}}^{L}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\big[{\bm{x}}_{\tau^{\prime}-L+1}\bar{{\bm{w}}}_{\tau}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}\big]\|=\|\begin{bmatrix}{\bm{C}}({\bm{A}}^{L}{\bm{\Sigma}}_{w})&{\bm{C}}{\bm{A}}({\bm{A}}^{L}{\bm{\Sigma}}_{w})&\ldots&{\bm{C}}{\bm{A}}^{L-1}({\bm{A}}^{L}{\bm{\Sigma}}_{w})\end{bmatrix}\|,

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\;\,\leq\|{\begin{bmatrix}{\bm{C}}&{\bm{C}}{\bm{A}}&\ldots&{\bm{C}}{\bm{A}}^{L-1}\end{bmatrix}}\|_{F}\|{\bm{A}}^{L}{\bm{\Sigma}}_{w}\|,

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\;\,\leq\phi({\bm{A}},\rho)\rho^{L}\|{{\bm{\Sigma}}_{w}}\|\|{{\bm{F}}}\|_{F}.

(A.25)

({\bm{d}})

When
\tau^{\prime}>\tau+L
, we have
