\displaystyle\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\big[{\bm{x}}_{\tau^{\prime}-L+1}\bar{{\bm{w}}}_{\tau}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}\big]=\sum_{i=\tau-L+1}^{\tau}{\bm{A}}^{\tau^{\prime}-L-i}\big({\bm{\updelta}}_{w}(\tau,i)\otimes{\bm{\Sigma}}_{w}\big)={\bm{A}}^{\tau^{\prime}-\tau-L}\begin{bmatrix}{\bm{\Sigma}}_{w}&{\bm{A}}{\bm{\Sigma}}_{w}&\ldots&{\bm{A}}^{L-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}_{w}\end{bmatrix}.

\displaystyle\|{\bm{C}}{\bm{A}}^{L}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\big[{\bm{x}}_{\tau^{\prime}-L+1}\bar{{\bm{w}}}_{\tau}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}\big]\|=\|\begin{bmatrix}{\bm{C}}({\bm{A}}^{\tau^{\prime}-\tau}{\bm{\Sigma}}_{w})&{\bm{C}}{\bm{A}}({\bm{A}}^{\tau^{\prime}-\tau}{\bm{\Sigma}}_{w})&\ldots&{\bm{C}}{\bm{A}}^{L-1}({\bm{A}}^{\tau^{\prime}-\tau}{\bm{\Sigma}}_{w})\end{bmatrix}\|,

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\;\,\leq\|{\begin{bmatrix}{\bm{C}}&{\bm{C}}{\bm{A}}&\ldots&{\bm{C}}{\bm{A}}^{L-1}\end{bmatrix}}\|_{F}\|{\bm{A}}^{\tau^{\prime}-\tau}{\bm{\Sigma}}_{w}\|,

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\;\,\leq\phi({\bm{A}},\rho)\rho^{L}\|{{\bm{\Sigma}}_{w}}\|\|{{\bm{F}}}\|_{F}(\rho^{\tau^{\prime}-\tau-L}).

(A.26)

Hence,
\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\tau^{\prime}=L\\
\tau^{\prime}\neq\tau\end{subarray}}^{T-1}\|{\bm{C}}{\bm{A}}^{L}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\big[{\bm{x}}_{\tau^{\prime}-L+1}\bar{{\bm{w}}}_{\tau}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}\big]\|\leq\big(L+\rho+\rho^{2}+\dots\big)\phi({\bm{A}},\rho)\rho^{L}\|{{\bm{\Sigma}}_{w}}\|\|{{\bm{F}}}\|_{F}
, which gives (i) above.

\bullet

Fourth summation in (
A.20
):

Similar to the previous summation, using Assumptions
1
and
2
, this term can also be upper bounded as,

\displaystyle\sum_{\tau=L}^{T-1}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\tau^{\prime}=L\\
\tau^{\prime}\neq\tau\end{subarray}}^{T-1}\big|\big[\big({\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{C}}{\bm{A}}^{L}

\displaystyle\sum_{i=0}^{\tau-L}{\bm{A}}^{\tau-L-i}\big({\bm{\updelta}}_{w}(\tau^{\prime},i)\otimes{\bm{\Sigma}}_{w}\big)\big)\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\big]\textup{{vec}}({\bm{F}})\big|\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}

\displaystyle\preceq\beta^{2}\|{{\bm{F}}}\|_{F}^{2}\|{\bm{\Sigma}}_{w}\|(L+\frac{\rho}{1-\rho})\phi({\bm{A}},\rho)\rho^{L}\sum_{\tau=L}^{T-1}\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}.

(A.27)

\blacksquare

Finalizing the proof:
Finally plugging (
A.21
), (
A.22
), (
A.23
), and (
A.27
) into (
A.20
), and combining the result with (
A.18
) and (
A.19
), we obtain the following upper bound,

\displaystyle\sum_{\tau=L}^{T-1}\sum_{\tau^{\prime}=L}^{T-1}

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{\zeta}[\tau,\tau^{\prime}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}]\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau^{\prime}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}{\bm{u}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}

\displaystyle\preceq\bigg[\sigma_{z}^{2}+\beta^{2}\big(\|{\bm{\Sigma}}_{w}\|\|{{\bm{F}}}\|_{F}^{2}+\|{\bm{C}}{\bm{A}}^{L}\|^{2}\|{\bm{\Gamma}}_{w}^{\infty}\|\big)+2\|{\bm{\Sigma}}_{w}\|\|{{\bm{F}}}\|_{F}^{2}\beta^{2}L

\displaystyle+2\beta^{2}\|{{\bm{C}}{\bm{A}}^{L}}\|^{2}\|{{\bm{\Gamma}}_{w}^{\infty}}\|\frac{\rho\phi({\bm{A}},\rho)}{1-\rho}+2\beta^{2}\|{{\bm{F}}}\|_{F}^{2}\|{\bm{\Sigma}}_{w}\|(L+\frac{\rho}{1-\rho})\phi({\bm{A}},\rho)\rho^{L}\bigg]\sum_{\tau=L}^{T-1}\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}},

\displaystyle=\bigg[\sigma_{z}^{2}+\|{\bm{\Sigma}}_{w}\|\|{{\bm{F}}}\|_{F}^{2}\beta^{2}\big(1+2L+2(L+\frac{\rho}{1-\rho})\phi({\bm{A}},\rho)\rho^{L}\big)

\displaystyle+\|{{\bm{\Gamma}}_{w}^{\infty}}\|\|{{\bm{C}}{\bm{A}}^{L}}\|^{2}\beta^{2}\big(1+2\frac{\rho\phi({\bm{A}},\rho)}{1-\rho}\big)\bigg]\sum_{\tau=L}^{T-1}\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}},

\displaystyle\preceq\bigg[\sigma_{z}^{2}+3\|{\bm{\Sigma}}_{w}\|\|{{\bm{F}}}\|_{F}^{2}\beta^{2}L\big(1+\frac{\phi({\bm{A}},\rho)\rho^{L}}{1-\rho}\ \big)+2\|{{\bm{\Gamma}}_{w}^{\infty}}\|\|{{\bm{C}}{\bm{A}}^{L}}\|^{2}\beta^{2}\frac{\phi({\bm{A}},\rho)}{1-\rho}\bigg]\sum_{\tau=L}^{T-1}\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}},

(A.28)

which is then combined with (
A.17
) to get the following upper bound on the conditional covariance of the estimation error,

\displaystyle{\bm{\Sigma}}[\Delta{\bm{G}}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}]

\displaystyle\qquad=\big(\sum_{\tau=L}^{T-1}\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\big)^{-1}\bigg(\sum_{\tau=L}^{T-1}\sum_{\tau^{\prime}=L}^{T-1}\mathcal{R}_{\zeta}[\tau,\tau^{\prime}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}]\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau^{\prime}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}{\bm{u}}_{\tau^{\prime}+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\bigg)\big(\sum_{\tau=L}^{T-1}\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\big)^{-1},

\displaystyle\qquad\preceq\bigg[\sigma_{z}^{2}+3\|{\bm{\Sigma}}_{w}\|\|{{\bm{F}}}\|_{F}^{2}\beta^{2}L\big(1+\frac{\phi({\bm{A}},\rho)\rho^{L}}{1-\rho}\ \big)+2\|{{\bm{\Gamma}}_{w}^{\infty}}\|\|{{\bm{C}}{\bm{A}}^{L}}\|^{2}\beta^{2}\frac{\phi({\bm{A}},\rho)}{1-\rho}\bigg]\big(\sum_{\tau=L}^{T-1}\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{\tau}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}{\bm{u}}_{\tau+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\big)^{-1}.

(A.29)

This completes the proof.

A.3
Proof of Lemma
6.3

Proof

To begin, recall that the estimation error is given by
\Delta{\bm{G}}=(\tilde{{\bm{U}}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\tilde{{\bm{U}}})^{-1}\tilde{{\bm{U}}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}(\tilde{{\bm{W}}}\textup{{vec}}({\bm{F}})+\tilde{{\bm{e}}}+{\bm{z}})
, where we define
\tilde{{\bm{W}}}\in\mathbb{R}^{(T-L)\times npL},\tilde{{\bm{e}}}\in\mathbb{R}^{(T-L)}
and
{\bm{z}}\in\mathbb{R}^{(T-L)}
as follows,

\displaystyle\tilde{{\bm{W}}}

\displaystyle:=\begin{bmatrix}\bar{{\bm{w}}}_{L}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{L+1}&\bar{{\bm{w}}}_{L+1}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{L+2}&\cdots&\bar{{\bm{w}}}_{T-1}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{T}\end{bmatrix}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}},

(A.30)

\displaystyle\tilde{{\bm{e}}}

\displaystyle:=\begin{bmatrix}{\bm{u}}_{L+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{e}}_{L+1}&{\bm{u}}_{L+2}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{e}}_{L+2}&\cdots&{\bm{u}}_{T}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{e}}_{T}\end{bmatrix}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}},\quad{\bm{z}}:=\begin{bmatrix}z_{L+1}&z_{L+2}&\cdots&z_{T}\end{bmatrix}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}.

Hence, using Assumption
2
, the conditionally expected estimation error is given by

\displaystyle\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[\Delta{\bm{G}}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}]\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(a)}}}{{=}}(\tilde{{\bm{U}}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\tilde{{\bm{U}}})^{-1}\tilde{{\bm{U}}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[\tilde{{\bm{e}}}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}],

(A.31)

where we get (a) from the observation that
\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[\tilde{{\bm{W}}}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}]=0
, and
\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[{\bm{z}}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}]=0
due to Assumption
2
. To proceed, let
\tilde{{\bm{U}}}=\tilde{{\bm{Q}}}\tilde{{\bm{\Sigma}}}\tilde{{\bm{R}}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}
be the singular value decomposition, where
\tilde{{\bm{Q}}}\in\mathbb{R}^{T-L\times p^{2}L}
. Then, from (
A.31
), we have

\displaystyle\|{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[\Delta{\bm{G}}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}]}\|_{\ell_{2}}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[\Delta{\bm{G}}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}]^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[\Delta{\bm{G}}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}]},

\displaystyle=\bigg[\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[\tilde{{\bm{e}}}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}]^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\tilde{{\bm{U}}}(\tilde{{\bm{U}}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\tilde{{\bm{U}}})^{-2}\tilde{{\bm{U}}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[\tilde{{\bm{e}}}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}]\bigg]^{1/2},

\displaystyle=\bigg[\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[\tilde{{\bm{e}}}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}]^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\tilde{{\bm{Q}}}\tilde{{\bm{\Sigma}}}\tilde{{\bm{R}}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}(\tilde{{\bm{R}}}\tilde{{\bm{\Sigma}}}\tilde{{\bm{Q}}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\tilde{{\bm{Q}}}\tilde{{\bm{\Sigma}}}\tilde{{\bm{R}}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}})^{-2}\tilde{{\bm{R}}}\tilde{{\bm{\Sigma}}}\tilde{{\bm{Q}}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[\tilde{{\bm{e}}}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}]\bigg]^{1/2},

\displaystyle=\bigg[\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[\tilde{{\bm{e}}}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}]^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\tilde{{\bm{Q}}}\tilde{{\bm{\Sigma}}}^{-2}\tilde{{\bm{Q}}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[\tilde{{\bm{e}}}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}]\bigg]^{1/2},

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(a)}}}{{\leq}}\bigg[\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[\tilde{{\bm{e}}}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}]^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\tilde{{\bm{Q}}}\tilde{{\bm{Q}}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[\tilde{{\bm{e}}}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}]\big/\lambda_{\min}(\tilde{{\bm{U}}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\tilde{{\bm{U}}})\bigg]^{1/2},

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(b)}}}{{\leq}}\frac{\|{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[\tilde{{\bm{e}}}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}]}\|_{\ell_{2}}}{\sqrt{\lambda_{\min}(\tilde{{\bm{U}}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\tilde{{\bm{U}}})}},

(A.32)

where we obtain (a) from the fact that
\|{\tilde{{\bm{\Sigma}}}^{-2}}\|=1/\lambda_{\min}(\tilde{{\bm{\Sigma}}}^{2})=1/\lambda_{\min}(\tilde{{\bm{U}}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\tilde{{\bm{U}}})
, and (b) from the observation that
\tilde{{\bm{Q}}}\tilde{{\bm{Q}}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}=\tilde{{\bm{U}}}(\tilde{{\bm{U}}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\tilde{{\bm{U}}})^{-1}\tilde{{\bm{U}}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}
is an orthogonal projection operator onto the column-space of
\tilde{{\bm{Q}}}
, hence,
\|{\tilde{{\bm{Q}}}\tilde{{\bm{Q}}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\|\leq 1
. To proceed, consider the
i
-th element of
\tilde{{\bm{e}}}
from (
A.30
), where
i\in[1,T-L]
. We have

\displaystyle|\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[[\tilde{{\bm{e}}}]_{i}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}]|

\displaystyle=|{\bm{u}}_{L+i}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[{\bm{e}}_{L+i}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}]|,

\displaystyle\leq\|{{\bm{u}}_{L+i}}\|_{\ell_{2}}\|{{\bm{C}}{\bm{A}}^{L}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[{\bm{x}}_{i}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}]}\|_{\ell_{2}},

\displaystyle\leq\beta\|{{\bm{C}}{\bm{A}}^{L}\sum_{k=0}^{i-1}{\bm{A}}^{k}{\bm{B}}{\bm{u}}_{i-k-1}}\|_{\ell_{2}},

\displaystyle\leq\beta\|{{\bm{C}}}\|\sum_{k=0}^{i-1}\|{{\bm{A}}^{L+k}}\|\|{\bm{B}}\|\|{{\bm{u}}_{i-k-1}}\|_{\ell_{2}},

\displaystyle\leq\beta^{2}\|{{\bm{B}}}\|\|{{\bm{C}}}\|\phi({\bm{A}},\rho)\rho^{L}\sum_{k=0}^{i-1}\rho^{k},

\displaystyle\leq\beta^{2}\|{{\bm{B}}}\|\|{{\bm{C}}}\|\phi({\bm{A}},\rho)\frac{\rho^{L}}{1-\rho}.

(A.33)

Combining this with (
A.32
), we obtain the following upper bound on the Frobenius norm of conditionally expected estimation error,

\displaystyle\|{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[\hat{{\bm{G}}}-{\bm{G}}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}]}\|_{F}=\|{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[\Delta{\bm{G}}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}]}\|_{\ell_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\sqrt{}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\sqrt{}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sqrt{}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sqrt{}$}}}}}\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.5pt\hbox{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.5pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\overline{\sum_{i=1}^{T-L}|\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[[\tilde{{\bm{e}}}]_{i}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}]|^{2}}$}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\raisebox{5.67776pt}{\hbox{}}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 2.5pt}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\raisebox{0.0pt}{\hbox{}}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\sqrt{\lambda_{\min}(\tilde{{\bm{U}}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\tilde{{\bm{U}}})}},

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\beta^{2}\|{{\bm{B}}}\|\|{{\bm{C}}}\|\phi({\bm{A}},\rho)\frac{\rho^{L}}{1-\rho}\sqrt{T-L}}{\sqrt{\lambda_{\min}(\tilde{{\bm{U}}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\tilde{{\bm{U}}})}}.

(A.34)

Similarly, we can also upper bound the weighted norm of the conditionally expected estimation error as follows,

\displaystyle\|{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[\Delta{\bm{G}}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}]}\|_{\tilde{{\bm{U}}}^{T}\tilde{{\bm{U}}}}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[\Delta{\bm{G}}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}]^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\tilde{{\bm{U}}}^{T}\tilde{{\bm{U}}}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[\Delta{\bm{G}}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}]},

\displaystyle=\bigg[\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[\tilde{{\bm{e}}}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}]^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\tilde{{\bm{U}}}(\tilde{{\bm{U}}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\tilde{{\bm{U}}})^{-1}\tilde{{\bm{U}}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[\tilde{{\bm{e}}}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}]\bigg]^{1/2},

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(a)}}}{{=}}\bigg[\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[\tilde{{\bm{e}}}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}]^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\tilde{{\bm{Q}}}\tilde{{\bm{\Sigma}}}\tilde{{\bm{R}}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}(\tilde{{\bm{R}}}\tilde{{\bm{\Sigma}}}\tilde{{\bm{Q}}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\tilde{{\bm{Q}}}\tilde{{\bm{\Sigma}}}\tilde{{\bm{R}}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}})^{-1}\tilde{{\bm{R}}}\tilde{{\bm{\Sigma}}}\tilde{{\bm{Q}}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[\tilde{{\bm{e}}}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}]\bigg]^{1/2},

\displaystyle=\bigg[\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[\tilde{{\bm{e}}}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}]^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\tilde{{\bm{Q}}}\tilde{{\bm{Q}}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[\tilde{{\bm{e}}}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}]\bigg]^{1/2},

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(b)}}}{{\leq}}\|{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[\tilde{{\bm{e}}}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{1:T}]}\|_{\ell_{2}},

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(c)}}}{{\leq}}\beta^{2}\|{{\bm{B}}}\|\|{{\bm{C}}}\|\phi({\bm{A}},\rho)\frac{\rho^{L}}{1-\rho}\sqrt{T-L},

(A.35)

where we get (a) from the singular value decomposition of
\tilde{{\bm{U}}}
, (b) from the observation that
\tilde{{\bm{Q}}}\tilde{{\bm{Q}}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}
is an orthogonal projection operator onto the column-space of
\tilde{{\bm{Q}}}
, hence,
\|{\tilde{{\bm{Q}}}\tilde{{\bm{Q}}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\|\leq 1
, and (c) from (
A.34
) above. This completes the proof.

A.4
Verifying Assumption
3
(b)

Suppose
\{{\bm{u}}_{t}\}_{t=0}^{T}\overset{\text{i.i.d.}}{\sim}\mathcal{N}(0,{\bm{I}}_{p})
. Then, for any
{\bm{v}}\in\mathcal{S}^{p^{2}L-1}
, we have

\displaystyle\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[({\bm{v}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\tilde{{\bm{u}}}_{t})^{4}]

\displaystyle=\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[((\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{t}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\otimes{\bm{u}}_{t+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}){\bm{v}})^{4}]=\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[({\bm{u}}_{t+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{V}}\bar{{\bm{u}}}_{t})^{4}]=\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\big[\big(\sum_{k=0}^{L-1}{\bm{u}}_{t+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{V}}_{k}{\bm{u}}_{t-k}\big)^{4}\big],

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=0}^{L-1}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[({\bm{u}}_{t+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{V}}_{k}{\bm{u}}_{t-k})^{4}]+3\sum_{k=0}^{L-1}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k^{\prime}=0\\
k^{\prime}\neq k\end{subarray}}^{L-1}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[({\bm{u}}_{t+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{V}}_{k}{\bm{u}}_{t-k})^{2}({\bm{u}}_{t+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{V}}_{k^{\prime}}{\bm{u}}_{t-k^{\prime}})^{2}],

(A.36)

where we obtain the first line by setting

\displaystyle{\bm{V}}:=\textup{{mtx}}({\bm{v}})=\begin{bmatrix}{\bm{V}}_{0}&{\bm{V}}_{1}&\cdots&{\bm{V}}_{L-1}\end{bmatrix}\in\mathbb{R}^{p\times pL}\quad\text{such that each}\quad{\bm{V}}_{k}\in\mathbb{R}^{p\times p}.

(A.37)

In the following, we will upper bound each expectation in (
A.36
) separately. Observing that

\displaystyle{\bm{u}}_{t+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{V}}_{k}{\bm{u}}_{t-k}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{t+1}\sim\mathcal{N}(0,\|{{\bm{V}}_{k}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{u}}_{t+1}}\|_{\ell_{2}}^{2}),

(A.38)

we have

\displaystyle\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[({\bm{u}}_{t+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{V}}_{k}{\bm{u}}_{t-k})^{4}]=\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\big[\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[({\bm{u}}_{t+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{V}}_{k}{\bm{u}}_{t-k})^{4}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{t+1}]\big]

\displaystyle=3\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[\|{{\bm{V}}_{k}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{u}}_{t+1}}\|_{\ell_{2}}^{4}],

\displaystyle=3\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[({\bm{u}}_{t+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{V}}_{k}{\bm{V}}_{k}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{u}}_{t+1})^{2}],

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(a)}}}{{=}}\textup{{tr}}({\bm{V}}_{k}{\bm{V}}_{k}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}})^{2}+2\textup{{tr}}(({\bm{V}}_{k}{\bm{V}}_{k}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}})^{2}),

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(b)}}}{{\leq}}\|{{\bm{V}}_{k}}\|_{F}^{4}+2\textup{{tr}}({\bm{V}}_{k}{\bm{V}}_{k}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}})^{2},

\displaystyle\leq 3\|{{\bm{V}}_{k}}\|_{F}^{4},

(A.39)

where we obtain (a) from
[
61
]
, and (b) from the fact that for any positive semi-definite matrix
{\bm{X}}
, we have
\textup{{tr}}({\bm{X}}^{2})\leq\textup{{tr}}({\bm{X}})^{2}
. Similarly, we also have

\displaystyle\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[({\bm{u}}_{t+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{V}}_{k}{\bm{u}}_{t-k})^{2}({\bm{u}}_{t+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{V}}_{k^{\prime}}{\bm{u}}_{t-k^{\prime}})^{2}]

\displaystyle=\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\big[\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[({\bm{u}}_{t+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{V}}_{k}{\bm{u}}_{t-k})^{2}({\bm{u}}_{t+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{V}}_{k^{\prime}}{\bm{u}}_{t-k^{\prime}})^{2}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{t+1}]\big],

\displaystyle=\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\big[\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[({\bm{u}}_{t+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{V}}_{k}{\bm{u}}_{t-k})^{2}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{t+1}]\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[({\bm{u}}_{t+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{V}}_{k^{\prime}}{\bm{u}}_{t-k^{\prime}})^{2}{~\big|~}{\bm{u}}_{t+1}]\big],

\displaystyle=\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[\|{{\bm{V}}_{k}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{u}}_{t+1}}\|_{\ell_{2}}^{2}\|{{\bm{V}}_{k^{\prime}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{u}}_{t+1}}\|_{\ell_{2}}^{2}],

\displaystyle=\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[({\bm{u}}_{t+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{V}}_{k}{\bm{V}}_{k}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{u}}_{t+1})({\bm{u}}_{t+1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{V}}_{k^{\prime}}{\bm{V}}_{k^{\prime}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{u}}_{t+1})],

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(a)}}}{{=}}\textup{{tr}}({\bm{V}}_{k}{\bm{V}}_{k}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}})\textup{{tr}}({\bm{V}}_{k^{\prime}}{\bm{V}}_{k^{\prime}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}})+2\textup{{tr}}({\bm{V}}_{k}{\bm{V}}_{k}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}{\bm{V}}_{k^{\prime}}{\bm{V}}_{k^{\prime}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}),

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(b)}}}{{\leq}}\|{{\bm{V}}_{k}}\|_{F}^{2}\|{{\bm{V}}_{k^{\prime}}}\|_{F}^{2}+2\sqrt{\textup{{tr}}(({\bm{V}}_{k}{\bm{V}}_{k}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}})^{2})\textup{{tr}}(({\bm{V}}_{k^{\prime}}{\bm{V}}_{k^{\prime}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}})^{2})},

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(c)}}}{{\leq}}3\|{{\bm{V}}_{k}}\|_{F}^{2}\|{{\bm{V}}_{k^{\prime}}}\|_{F}^{2},

(A.40)

where we obtain (a) from
[
61
]
, (b) from Cauchy-Schwarz inequality applied to the trace of the product of two positive semi-definite matrices, and (c) from the fact that for any positive semi-definite matrix
{\bm{X}}
, we have
\textup{{tr}}({\bm{X}}^{2})\leq\textup{{tr}}({\bm{X}})^{2}
. Finally, combining (
A.39
) and (
A.40
) into (
A.36
), we obtain out final result,

\displaystyle\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[({\bm{v}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\tilde{{\bm{u}}}_{t})^{4}]

\displaystyle=3\sum_{k=0}^{L-1}\|{{\bm{V}}_{k}}\|_{F}^{4}+9\sum_{k=0}^{L-1}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k^{\prime}=0\\
k^{\prime}\neq k\end{subarray}}^{L-1}\|{{\bm{V}}_{k}}\|_{F}^{2}\|{{\bm{V}}_{k^{\prime}}}\|_{F}^{2},

\displaystyle\leq 9\big(\sum_{k=0}^{L-1}\|{{\bm{V}}_{k}}\|_{F}^{2}\big)^{2}\leq 9.

(A.41)

Hence, Assumption
3
(
{\bm{b}}
) holds for Gaussian inputs with
m_{4}=9
.
